# 吴恩达《AI for Everyone》给所有人的AI课 — 完整逐字稿（中英对照·含大纲）

**AI for Everyone — Full Transcript (Bilingual, with Outlines)**

---

## 目录

- **一、课程介绍——这门非技术课程将带你穿透AI的炒作迷雾，建立对AI能力和局限的真实认知，让你比大多数大公司CEO更懂AI**
  *7 个小节*

- **二、机器学习——监督学习就是让AI学习从A到B的映射，这项技术是ChatGPT等大语言模型的核心，而它近年爆发的关键在于"大数据+大模型"**
  *7 个小节*

- **三、什么是数据——数据是AI的燃料，但获取数据有四种方式，使用数据有两个常见误区，而且数据往往是脏的，需要AI团队来清洗和处理**
  *7 个小节*

- **四、人工智能术语解析——机器学习、数据科学、深度学习、神经网络到底是什么意思，它们之间是什么关系，搞清楚这些你就不会再被各种Buzzword搞晕了**
  *7 个小节*

- **五、人工智能公司的核心要素——用几个神经网络不叫AI公司，真正的AI公司是系统性做好AI让你能做的事：战略数据获取、统一数据仓库、发现自动化机会、引入新角色，以及一套五步AI转型指南**
  *7 个小节*

- **六、机器学习的能力与局限——AI不是魔法，一个实用的判断法则是"你一秒钟内能做出的判断，AI大概也能做"，但它搞不定股票预测这种高度随机的事情**
  *5 个小节*

- **七、更多机器学习示例——AI能检测其他车辆但看不懂人的手势，能从X光片诊断肺炎但没法像人一样读教科书学知识，而且它对训练数据之外的新类型数据适应能力远远不如人类**
  *5 个小节*

- **八、深度学习解释（第1部分）——神经网络就像搭乐高积木，每个神经元计算一个简单函数，堆叠起来就能算出极其复杂的A到B映射，而且中间那些层全是你不需要操心的——AI自己搞定**
  *4 个小节*

- **九、深度学习解释（第2部分）——人脸识别中，神经元自动从像素中逐层提取特征：先检测边缘，再检测眼鼻嘴等器官，再到脸型，最后输出身份，整个过程你只需要提供图片和答案，中间的每一步AI自己搞定**
  *3 个小节*

- **二、第2周课程介绍——上周学了AI是什么，这周教你如何把AI技术落地成实际项目：从项目工作流程、项目选择框架到数据和团队组织，学完你就能和朋友一起尝试有意思的AI项目了**
  *3 个小节*

- **三、机器学习项目工作流程——机器学习项目有三个核心步骤：收集数据、训练模型、部署模型，整个过程充满迭代，以语音识别和自动驾驶为例一步步拆解给你看**
  *4 个小节*

- **四、数据科学项目工作流程——与机器学习不同，数据科学的输出是一组可操作的洞察，帮你做不同的决策；三步走：收集数据、分析数据、提出假设和行动建议，以销售漏斗优化和咖啡杯生产线优化两个案例详细拆解**
  *5 个小节*

- **五、每个工作职能都被数据改变——从销售、制造、招聘、营销到农业，数据和AI已经渗透到每一个岗位，用具体案例看数据科学和机器学习如何影响你和你同事的工作**
  *6 个小节*

- **六、如何选择AI项目（上）——选AI项目要找"AI能做"和"业务有价值"的交集；用三个原则做头脑风暴：自动化任务而非工作、围绕业务价值驱动因素、围绕业务痛点，并且不要被"大数据"吓到，小数据集也能起步**
  *5 个小节*

- **七、如何选择AI项目（下）——确定项目前要做技术尽调和业务尽调；还要加一道伦理尽调确保对人类社会有益；另外要面对"自建还是采购"的经典选择，记住"别在火车前面跑"**
  *5 个小节*

- **八、与AI团队协作——帮AI团队成功的关键：用统计方式定义验收标准、理解训练集和测试集的分工、不要期望100%准确率；即使没有AI团队，你和工程师朋友上几门在线课程也能起步**
  *5 个小节*

- **九、AI团队技术工具（可选）——了解AI工程师常用的开源框架（PyTorch、TensorFlow、HuggingFace等）、论文发布平台Arxiv、代码托管平台GitHub；理解CPU/GPU/TPU的区别；以及云端部署、本地部署和边缘部署三种方案**
  *4 个小节*

- **十、第3周课程介绍——前两周学了AI是什么和怎么建AI项目，这周学习AI项目如何在公司背景下发挥作用：复杂AI产品案例研究、AI团队角色、AI转型指南，以及你能立刻迈出的第一步**
  *3 个小节*

- **一、智能音箱案例研究——构建复杂AI产品不是用单一算法做A到B映射，而是把多个AI组件串联成"AI流水线"，每一步都解决一个小问题，最终协同完成语音指令**
  *3 个小节*

- **一、自动驾驶汽车案例研究——自动驾驶的AI系统远比"用摄像头看路"复杂，它由车辆检测、行人检测、轨迹预测、运动规划等多个AI组件组成，每一块都需要专门的团队开发，最终拼在一起才能安全行驶**
  *3 个小节*

- **一、AI团队角色解析——大型AI团队通常包含软件工程师、机器学习工程师、机器学习研究员、应用机器学习科学家、数据科学家、数据工程师和AI产品经理，但哪怕你只有一两个人也能起步做AI项目**
  *4 个小节*

- **一、AI转型指南第1部分——帮助一家优秀公司变成AI优秀公司需要分五步：执行试点项目获取动能、建立内部AI团队、提供广泛的AI培训、制定AI战略、做内外沟通，前三个步骤是打基础的关键**
  *4 个小节*

- **一、AI转型指南第2部分——AI战略不是第一步而是第四步，先去亲手做AI项目有了体感再定战略；同时要善于利用"AI的正向循环"（更好产品->更多用户->更多数据->更好产品）在垂直领域建立壁垒，并通过内外沟通赢得投资人和政府等利益相关者的支持**
  *4 个小节*

- **一、AI实践的5个"不要"——现实中很多公司踩过的坑包括：期望AI解决一切、只招两三个机器学习工程师就指望他们想到所有用例、期望AI项目一次成功、照搬传统项目规划流程、认为没有明星AI工程师就什么都做不了；关键是"先做起来"，第二个项目比第一个好，第三个比第二个好**
  *3 个小节*

- **一、迈出AI第一步——完整的AI转型可能需要两三年，但重要的是你能迈出第一步，而且你上这门课本身就已经是很好的第一步了；从找朋友一起学习、头脑风暴小项目、到招聘AI人才、推动公司AI转型，无论什么起点都能开始**
  *3 个小节*

- **一、主要AI应用领域概览——从图像/视频、语言、语音、生成式AI到机器人，AI已成功应用于多个领域；监督学习不仅在非结构化数据（图片、语音、文本）上大放异彩，在结构化数据（表格数据）上创造的经济价值至少同等巨大，只是后者不如图片识别那样"看得见"所以媒体报道少很多**
  *5 个小节*

- **一、主要AI技术概览——除监督学习外，还有无监督学习、迁移学习、强化学习、GANs和知识图谱等技术；监督学习虽然创造了最大经济价值，但其他技术各有独特优势，知道它们的存在能帮你更好地跟AI工程师交流**
  *6 个小节*

- **一、第4周课程介绍——AI是一种让小团队也能影响亿万人生活的"超能力"，最后一周将讨论AI的社会影响：偏见、对抗性攻击、发展中经济体和就业**
  *3 个小节*

- **一、AI的"金凤花规则"——对AI既不能过分乐观，也不能过分悲观，取中间的务实态度刚刚好；把通用人工智能（AGI）或超级智能当作"近在眼前"是导致过度乐观和过度恐惧的共同根源**
  *4 个小节*

- **一、AI偏见与歧视——AI从互联网文本中学习，会把人类语言中的不健康刻板印象也学进去：它认为"男人之于程序员，正如女人之于家庭主妇"；好消息是我们现在对减少AI偏见的思路比减少人类偏见更清晰**
  *5 个小节*

- **一、对抗性攻击——人类肉眼几乎看不出任何变化的微小像素扰动，就能让AI把蜂鸟认成锤子、把野兔认成桌子、把香蕉认成烤面包机，甚至贴上贴纸就能让自动驾驶汽车看不见停车标志**
  *4 个小节*

- **一、AI的恶意应用——Deepfake换脸造假、AI生成假评论、压迫性监控……AI给恶意使用者也提供了新武器；但就像反垃圾邮件一方资源远超垃圾邮件发送者一样，"善的一方"整体资源占优，最终会占上风**
  *3 个小节*

- **一、AI与发展中经济体——发展中经济体过去靠"经济阶梯"一步步往上爬（从农业到纺织到制造业到电子到汽车），但AI自动化可能把底下几级台阶敲掉；而AI也可以成为"弹跳床"，让一些国家跳过台阶直接跳到更高的位置**
  *4 个小节*

- **一、AI与就业——麦肯锡估计到2030年AI自动化将替代4到8亿个工作岗位，但AI新创造的岗位数量可能更多；生成式AI的出现让以前"不太怕自动化"的写作沟通类工作也面临AI替代风险**
  *5 个小节*

- **一、课程总结——四周时间，你从AI技术原理、AI项目构建到公司AI转型、AI社会影响，系统地完成了从"AI小白"到比大多数大公司CEO更懂AI的蜕变**
  *5 个小节*

## 一、课程介绍——这门非技术课程将带你穿透AI的炒作迷雾，建立对AI能力和局限的真实认知，让你比大多数大公司CEO更懂AI

### 这是一门面向所有人的非技术AI课程——不管你是想搞懂AI术语背后的真正含义、在个人或公司场景中使用AI，还是想理解AI对社会的影响并找到应对之道，这门课都会教你；第一周的任务就是穿透炒作，给你一个关于AI是什么的真实图景

> 欢迎来到《AI for everyone》。AI 正在改变我们工作和生活的方式，这门非技术课程将教会你如何在 AI 崛起的大潮中找准自己的方向。不管你是想搞清楚那些流行术语到底是什么意思，还是想在个人场景或公司等组织里亲自用上 AI，这门课都会教你怎么做。如果你想了解 AI 如何影响社会以及如何应对，这门课也会告诉你。第一周，我们会先穿透炒作，给你一个关于 AI 到底是什么的真实认知。咱们开始吧。
> Welcome to AI for everyone. AI is changing the way we work and live. And this non-technical course will teach you how to navigate the rise of AI. Whether you want to know what's behind the buzzwords, or whether you want to perhaps use AI yourself, either in a personal context or in a corporation or other organization, this course will teach you how. And if you want to understand how AI is affecting society and how you can navigate that, you also learn that from this course. In this first week, we'll start by cutting through the hype and giving you a realistic view of what AI really is. Let's get started.

### 麦肯锡的研究预测，到2033年AI每年将创造13到22万亿美元的额外价值——其中3到4万亿来自生成式AI（能生成高质量文字、图像和音频的新技术），但更大的一部分价值来自更成熟的AI技术，比如监督学习；而且AI已经给软件行业带来了巨大价值，未来更大的机会其实在软件行业之外

> 很多专家都认同，AI 将创造巨大的价值。比如，麦肯锡全球研究院的一项研究显示，到 2033 年，AI 每年将额外创造 13 到 22 万亿美元的价值。在这 13 到 22 万亿中，有 3 到 4 万亿美元预计来自所谓的生成式 AI——这是一种相对较新的 AI 技术，能够生成高质量的内容，特别是文字、图像和音频。但更大的一部分价值将来自生成式 AI 以外的其他 AI 形式，比如一种叫监督学习的技术。这门课会更侧重这些更成熟的 AI 技术。AI 已经在软件行业创造了巨大价值。
> Many experts agree that AI will create a huge amount of value. For example, according to a study by McKinsey Global Institute, AI will create an additional 13 to 22 trillion US dollars of value annually by the year 2033. Of this 13 to 22 trillion, 3 to 4 trillion dollars is predicted to come from what's called generative AI, which is a relatively new type of AI technology that can produce high quality content, specifically text, images and audio. But the larger portion of value will be from other forms of AI than generative AI. For example, a technology called supervised learning will focus more on these other more mature types of AI technology in this course. AI is already creating tremendous amounts of value in the software industry.

### AI的影响将远超软件行业，渗透到零售、旅游、交通、汽车、材料、制造等几乎每一个领域——吴恩达和他的朋友曾互相挑战说出一个AI不会有巨大影响的行业，他想到的最好例子是美发行业，结果一位机器人学教授朋友当场反驳说：大多数人的发型机器人确实剪不了，但安德鲁你的发型，机器人绝对能搞定

> 麦肯锡的研究还指出，未来大量价值将诞生在软件行业之外，比如零售、旅游、交通、汽车、材料、制造等行业。我几乎想不出有哪个行业，AI 在未来几年不会产生巨大影响。我和朋友们曾经互相挑战，要说出一个你觉得 AI 不会有很大影响的行业。我能想到的最好例子是美发行业——你知道，怎么用 AI 机器人来剪头发嘛。但我有次在台上说了这个观点，一位搞机器人学的教授朋友正好在台下。她当场站起来，直视着我说：安德鲁，大多数人的发型，我确实没法让机器人剪出来。但她看着我说：但是你的发型嘛，安德鲁，机器人搞得定。
> And the McKinsey study points out that a lot of the value to be created in the future lies outside the software industry. For example, in sectors such as retail, travel, transportation, automotive, materials, manufacturing, and so on. I should have a hard time thinking of an industry that I don't think AI will have a huge impact on in the next several years. My friends and I used to challenge each other to name an industry where we don't think AI will have a huge impact. And my best example was the hairdressing industry because you know how to use AI robotics to automate hairdressing. But I once said this on stage and one of my friends who is a robotics professor was in the audience that day. And she actually stood up and she looked at me in the eye and she said, you know, Andrew, most people's hairstyles, I couldn't get a robot to cut their hair that way. But she looked me and said, your hairstyle, Andrew, that a robot can do.

### AI热潮背后有一个重要的概念区分——你今天看到的大量AI价值来自"弱人工智能"（ANI），也就是只会做一件事的"一招鲜"，比如智能音箱、自动驾驶、网页搜索；而ChatGPT这类生成式AI的出现让AI变得更通用了一些；至于能做任何人类智力任务的"通用人工智能"（AGI），虽然研究在一步步往前挪，但我们离它还有几十年甚至上百年——别被那些"邪恶机器人统治人类"的炒作吓到了

> 人们对 AI 有很多兴奋，但也有很多不必要的炒作。原因之一是 AI 其实是两个不同的概念。我们今天看到的大部分 AI 价值来自"弱人工智能"——Artificial Narrow Intelligence，简称 ANI。这些 AI 只会做一件事，比如智能音箱、自动驾驶汽车、网页搜索用的 AI，或者农业和工厂里的 AI 应用。这类 AI 是"一招鲜"——但当你找到了合适的那一招，它就能创造巨大的价值。而随着生成式 AI 的崛起，像 ChatGPT 和 Bard 这类工具的出现，AI 开始变得更通用了一些。比如 ChatGPT 可以做文字编辑、头脑风暴伙伴、文本摘要，还能帮你完成很多其他任务。这些模型是一个令人兴奋的进展，进一步拓展了我们用 AI 能做的事情。此外，AI 还指代另一个概念——AGI，也就是通用人工智能。这是要造出能做人类能做的任何智力任务的 AI，甚至可能是超级智能，比任何人类能做的事都多。我看到弱人工智能和生成式 AI 在飞速进步，AI 研究也在一小步一小步地朝 AGI 靠近，这很让人激动。但现实是，我们离 AGI 还非常非常远。不幸的是，弱人工智能和生成式 AI 的快速进步——它们确实非常有价值——让人们得出"AI 在飞速发展"的结论，这倒没错。但人们又进一步错误地认为，我们可能快要实现 AGI 了，这就导致了一些过度膨胀的、不必要的恐惧，比如"有意识的邪恶机器人要来统治人类"之类的。我认为 AGI 是研究者的一个激动人心的目标，但在到达那里之前还需要很多次技术突破。可能要几十年，甚至上百年——希望如此，但我不确定我们这辈子能不能看到。既然 AGI 还那么遥远，我觉得没必要为它过分焦虑。
> There's a lot of excitement, but also a lot of unnecessary hype about AI. One of the reasons for this is because AI is actually two separate ideas. A large amount of the value we see from AI today is artificial narrow intelligence or ANI. These are AIs that do one thing such as a smart speaker or a self-driving car or AI for a web search or AI applications in farming or in a factory. These types of AI are one trick ponies. But when you find the appropriate trick, this can be incredibly valuable. With the rise of generative AI, things like ChatGPT and Bard, we're also starting to see AI does a bit more general purpose. For example, ChatGPT can be a copy editor, brainstorming partner, text summarizer, and help with many other tasks. These models have been an exciting development and this further expanding what we can now do with AI. In addition, AI also refers to the concept of AGI or artificial general intelligence. This is the goal of building AI that could do any intellectual tasks that a human can or maybe even be super intelligent and do even more things than any human can. I'm seeing tons of progress in ANI, artificial narrow intelligence, as well as on generative AI. And it feels like AI research is slowly taking baby steps, tiny baby steps, toward AGI, which is exciting. But realistically, we're still very far from AGI or artificial general intelligence. Unfortunately, the rapid progress in ANI and generative AI, which are incredibly valuable, that has caused people to conclude that there's a lot of progress in AI, which is actually true. But that in turn has caused people to falsely think that we might be on the verge of AGI as well, which is leading to some overblown and unnecessary fears about evil sentient robots coming to take over humanity. I think AGI is an exciting goal for researchers to work on, but it'll take multiple technological breakthroughs before we get there. And it may be decades, maybe even hundreds of years, I hope, but I'm not sure if we will get there in our lifetimes. But given how far away AGI is, I think there is no need to undue the anxiety about it.

### 第一周的课程安排：首先搞清楚AI到底是什么——你将学习机器学习的含义、什么是数据以及哪些数据有价值、什么让一家公司成为"AI公司"、以及最重要的是——机器学习能做什么和不能做什么；媒体和研究论文只报道成功案例，而你要看到失败案例才能做出准确判断

> 在这一周，你将学到弱人工智能能做什么、怎么把它们用到你自己的问题里。通过案例研究，你还会看到这些"一招鲜"的弱人工智能怎么被用来构建真正有价值的应用，比如智能音箱和自动驾驶汽车。具体来说，这周你会学到：AI 是什么？你可能听说过机器学习，下个视频就会教你什么是机器学习。你还会学到什么是数据，哪些类型的数据有价值，哪些没有。你会学到什么让一家公司成为"AI 公司"或"AI 优先公司"，这样也许你可以开始思考有什么办法提升你的公司或其他组织使用 AI 的能力。更重要的是，这周你还会学到机器学习能做什么、不能做什么。在我们的社会中，报纸和研究论文往往只谈论机器学习和 AI 的成功故事，我们几乎看不到任何失败案例，因为失败案例没那么有报道价值。但为了让你对 AI 和机器学习的能力与局限有一个真实的认知，我认为你必须两类案例都看到，这样才能更准确地判断哪些事适合用这些技术去做，哪些可能不适合。
> In this week, you learn what ANI can do and how to apply them to your problems. Through case studies, you also see how ANI, these one trick ponies can be used to build really valuable applications, such as smart speakers and self-driving cars. In detail, in this week, you learn what is AI. You may have heard of machine learning and the next video will teach you what is machine learning. You also learn what is data and what types of data are valuable, but also what types of data are not valuable. You learn what it is that makes a company, an AI company, or an AI first company, so that perhaps you can start thinking if there are ways to improve your company or other organization's ability to use AI. And importantly, you also learn this week what machine learning can and cannot do. In our society, newspapers, as well as research papers, tend to talk only about the success stories of machine learning and AI. And we hardly ever see any failure stories because they just aren't as interesting to report on. But for you to have a realistic view of what AI and what machine learning can and cannot do, I think it's important that you see examples of both so that you can make more accurate judgments about what you may and maybe should not try to use these technologies for.

### 深度学习（也叫神经网络）是近年机器学习崛起的核心驱动力——本周最后两个可选视频会给你一个关于深度学习的直观解释；第二周你将学习如何构建AI项目并选择技术上可行且有价值的项目，第三周学习如何在你公司内部建立AI能力，第四周探讨AI的偏见、对发展中经济体和就业的影响等社会议题

> 最后，机器学习的这波崛起很大程度上是由深度学习的兴起驱动的——深度学习有时也叫神经网络。在本周最后的两个可选视频中，你会看到一个关于深度学习的直观解释，这样你会更好地理解它们能做什么，尤其是一系列弱人工智能任务。这就是你第一周要学的内容。第一周结束时，你将对 AI 技术以及它们能做什么、不能做什么有一个清晰的认识。第二周，你将学习如何用这些 AI 技术来构建有价值的项目。你会了解做 AI 项目是什么感觉，以及你应该怎样确保你选择的项目在技术上是可行的，同时对你、你的公司或组织是有价值的。学完怎么构建 AI 项目之后，第三周你将学习怎么在你的公司里应用 AI。特别是，如果你想朝"让公司擅长 AI"这个方向迈出几步，你会看到《AI 转型指南》，学会如何组建 AI 团队以及构建复杂的 AI 产品。最后，AI 对社会正产生巨大影响。在第四周也就是最后一周，你会学习 AI 系统如何产生偏见，以及如何减少或消除这些偏见。你还会学习 AI 如何影响发展中经济体、如何影响就业，从而能更好地为自己和你的组织应对 AI 的崛起。
> Finally, a lot of the recent rise of machine learning has been driven through the rise of deep learning, sometimes also called neural networks. In the final two optional videos of this week, you can also see an intuitive explanation of deep learning so that you will better understand what they can do, particularly for a set of narrow AI tasks. So that's what you learn this week. And by the end of this week, you have a sense of AI technologies and what they can and cannot do. In the second week, you learn how these AI technologies can be used to build valuable projects. You learn what it feels like to build an AI project, as well as what you should do to make sure you select projects that are technically feasible, as well as valuable to you or your business or other organization. After learning what it takes to build AI projects, in the third week, you learn how to build AI in your company. In particular, if you want to take a few steps toward making your company good at AI, you see the AI Transformation Playbook and learn how to build AI teams and also build complex AI products. Finally, AI is having a huge impact on society. In the fourth and final week, you learn about how AI systems can be biased and how to diminish or eliminate such biases. You also learn how AI is affecting developing economies and how AI is affecting jobs and be better able to navigate this rise of AI for yourself and for your organization.

### 完成这四周课程后，你对AI技术及其应用的理解将超过大多数大公司的CEO——你将有能力帮助自己和你的组织应对AI的崛起，甚至为他人提供指导；驱动这一波AI浪潮的核心技术是机器学习，下一节课就来讲讲到底什么是机器学习

> 完成这四周课程后，你对 AI 技术的理解、以及帮助自己或公司或其他组织应对 AI 崛起的能力，将超过大多数大公司的 CEO。所以我希望这门课结束后，你能在别人应对这些问题时，为他们提供指导。驱动近期 AI 崛起的主要技术之一就是机器学习。但机器学习到底是什么？下个视频我们来一探究竟。
> By the end of this four week course, you'll be more knowledgeable and better qualified than even the CEOs of most large companies in terms of your understanding of AI technology, as well as your ability to help yourself or help your company or other organization navigate the rise of AI. And so I hope that after this course, you'll be in a position to provide leadership to others as well as they navigate these issues. Now, one of the major technologies driving the recent rise of AI is machine learning. But what is machine learning? Let's take a look in the next video.

## 二、机器学习——监督学习就是让AI学习从A到B的映射，这项技术是ChatGPT等大语言模型的核心，而它近年爆发的关键在于"大数据+大模型"

### 机器学习中最常用的一种叫监督学习——它的本质就是学习从输入A到输出B的映射关系；比如输入一封邮件（A），输出它是不是垃圾邮件（0或1），这就是垃圾邮件过滤器的核心；输入一段音频（A），输出对应的文字（B），就是语音识别；输入英文（A），输出中文（B），就是机器翻译；输入广告信息加用户信息（A），输出你会不会点击（B），就是在线广告——这可能是最赚钱的监督学习应用，虽然不太酷，但经济价值巨大

> 机器学习中最常用的一种叫监督学习，也就是学习从A到B、从输入到输出的映射。来看几个例子。如果输入A是一封邮件，你想要的输出B是这封邮件是不是垃圾邮件——是就是1，不是就是0——那这就是垃圾邮件过滤器的核心AI。或者输入A是一段音频，AI的任务是输出对应的文字，那就是语音识别。再比如，输入英文、输出另一种语言比如中文或西班牙语，这就是机器翻译。监督学习中可能最赚钱的一种应用是在线广告——所有大的在线广告平台都有一段AI，输入广告的相关信息和你的相关信息，然后判断你会不会点击这个广告。通过向你展示你最可能点击的广告，这个商业模式非常赚钱。也许不是最激动人心的应用，但它今天确实产生了巨大的经济影响。

> The most commonly used type of machine learning is the type of AI that learns A to B or input to output mappings. This is called supervised learning. Let's see some examples. If the input A is an email and the output B you want is this email spam one out, zero one, then this is the core piece of AI used to build a spam filter. Or if the input A is an audio clip and the AI's job is to output the text transfer, then this is speech recognition. More examples, if you want to input English and have it output a different language, Chinese, Spanish, something else, then this is machine translation. Or the most lucrative form of supervised learning of this type of machine learning may be online advertising, where all the large online ad platforms have a piece of AI that inputs some information about an ad and some information about you and tries to figure out whether you click on this ad or not. And by showing you the ad so you're most likely to click on, this turns out to be very lucrative. Maybe not the most inspiring application, but certainly having a huge economic impact today.

### 监督学习还可以用于自动驾驶——输入摄像头图像和雷达等传感器信息（A），输出其他车辆的位置（B），这样你的车就可以避开其他车辆；在制造业中，输入一张刚下线的手机照片（A），输出有没有划痕、凹痕等缺陷（B），这就是视觉检测，帮助制造商减少或防止产品缺陷

> 如果你想造一辆自动驾驶汽车，其中一个关键的AI组件是这样一段AI：它输入一张图像和来自雷达或其他传感器的信息，然后输出其他车辆的位置，这样你的自动驾驶汽车就能避开它们。在制造业中——我在制造业做了很多工作——你输入一张刚制造出来的产品的照片，比如一部刚下流水线的手机的照片（这是手机的照片，不是用手机拍的照片），然后你想输出的是这个东西上有没有划痕、有没有凹痕或者其他缺陷。这就是视觉检测，它帮助制造商减少或防止产品缺陷。

> Or if you want to build a self-driving car, one of the key pieces of AI is an AI that takes its input in an image and some information from the radar or from other sensors and outputs the position of other cars so your self-driving car can avoid the other cars. Or in manufacturing, I've actually done a lot of work in manufacturing where you would take as input a picture of something you've just manufactured, such as a picture of a cell phone coming off an assembly line. This is a picture of a phone, not a picture taken by a phone. And you want to output is there a scratch, is there a dent, or some other defect on this thing you've just manufactured. And this is visual inspection, which helps manufacturers reduce or prevent defects in the things that they're making.

### 像ChatGPT这样的生成式AI系统，其核心也是监督学习——大语言模型（LLM）通过从海量互联网文本中学习，训练出一个能反复预测下一个词的模型；比如"我最喜欢的饮料是荔枝珍珠奶茶"这句话，会被拆成多个A→B数据点："我最喜欢的饮料"→预测"是"，"我最喜欢的饮料是"→预测"荔枝"，依此类推；用数千亿甚至上万亿个词训练出来的超大模型，给它一个提示（prompt），就能生成非常流畅的后续文字

> 监督学习也是ChatGPT这类生成式AI聊天机器人的核心。这些系统通过从海量文本中学习——从互联网上下载的文本——当你给它几个词作为输入时，模型就能预测接下来应该出现的下一个词。这些模型叫大语言模型（LLM），它们通过反复预测下一个应该输出的词来生成新的文字。鉴于大语言模型受到的广泛关注，我们简单看看它们是怎么工作的。大语言模型是用监督学习训练出来的——训练一个模型反复预测下一个词。举个例子，如果一个AI系统在互联网上读到过这样一句话："我最喜欢的饮料是荔枝珍珠奶茶"，那么这一句话就会被转化成很多个A到B的数据点，让模型学习预测下一个词。具体来说，给定这句话，我们就有了一个数据点：给定"我最喜欢的饮料"，你预测下一个词是什么？正确答案是"是"。然后给定"我最喜欢的饮料是"，你预测下一个词是什么？正确答案是"荔枝"。以此类推，直到句子里所有词都用完。一句话被拆成了很多个输入A和输出B的数据点。当你用大量数据——比如数千亿甚至超过一万亿个词——训练一个非常大的AI系统时，你就得到了像ChatGPT这样的大语言模型，给它一段初始文字也就是prompt，它就能非常擅长地生成后续的文字。

> Supervised learning also lies at the heart of generative AI systems like ChatGPT and chatbots that generate text. These systems work by learning from huge amounts of text, downloaded from the internet, so that when given a few words as the input, the model can predict the next word that comes after. These models, which are called large language models or LLMs, generate new text by repeatedly predicting what is the next word they should output. Given the widespread attention on LLMs, let's look briefly at how they work. Large language models are built by using supervised learning to train a model to repeatedly predict the next word. For example, if an AI system has read on the internet a sentence like "my favorite drink is Lychee bubble tea", then the single sentence would be turned into a lot of A to B data points for the model to learn to predict the next word. Specifically, given this sentence, we now have one data point that says, given the phrase "my favorite drink", what do you predict is the next word? In this case, the right answer is "is". And given "my favorite drink is", what do you predict is the next word? And the correct answer is "Lychee", and so on, until you have used all the words in the sentence. So this one sentence is turned into multiple inputs A and outputs B. When you train a very large AI system on a lot of data, say hundreds of billions or even over a trillion words, then you get a large language model like ChatGPT that, given an initial piece of text called a prompt, is very good at generating some additional words in response to that prompt.

### 我这里的描述省略了一些技术细节——比如模型怎么学会遵循指令而不只是预测互联网上的下一个词，以及开发者怎么让模型不太可能输出歧视性内容或有害指令；如果你想深入了解，可以去看我的《Generative AI for Everyone》课程；但LLM的核心就是监督学习——从海量数据中学习预测下一个词

> 我这里的描述确实省略了一些技术细节，比如模型是怎么学会遵循指令的——而不仅仅是预测互联网上找到的下一个词——以及开发者是怎么让模型不太可能生成不恰当的输出，比如带有歧视的内容或有害指令。如果你感兴趣，可以去看《Generative AI for Everyone》那门课了解更多细节。但大语言模型的核心，就是这项用监督学习从海量数据中学习预测下一个词的技术。

> The description I presented here does omit some technical details like how the model learns to follow instructions rather than just predict the next word found on the internet, and also how developers make the model less likely to generate inappropriate outputs, such as one that exhibit discrimination or hand out harmful instructions. If you're interested, you can learn more about these details in the course Generative AI for Everyone. At the heart of LLMs though, is this technology that learns from a lot of data to predict what is the next word using supervised learning.

### 总结一下：监督学习就是学习输入到输出、A到B的映射——虽然A到B听起来很局限，但当你找到合适的应用场景，它就能创造巨大价值；这个想法已经存在几十年了，但为什么最近几年才爆发？关键在于一张图——横轴是每个任务的数据量，纵轴是AI系统的性能，传统AI给再多数据性能也会很快饱和，而用神经网络或深度学习后，模型越大、数据越多，性能就持续往上涨

> 总结一下，监督学习就是学习输入到输出、A到B的映射。一方面，输入输出A到B听起来好像很局限，但当你找到合适的应用场景，这就能变得非常有价值。监督学习这个想法其实已经存在几十年了，但它真正起飞是在最近几年。为什么？我的朋友经常问我：安德鲁，为什么监督学习现在才火起来？我给他们画一张图，现在我也给你看看这张图，这样你以后也可以画给别人解释。假设横轴是你每个任务拥有的数据量——比如对语音识别来说，就是你有多少音频数据和对应的文字记录。在很多行业，过去二十年我们能获取的数据量大幅增长。互联网的兴起、电脑的普及，很多以前写在纸上的东西现在都记录在数字电脑上了。所以我们手上的数据越来越多、越来越多。然后假设纵轴是AI系统的性能。结果是，如果你用传统AI系统，性能增长大概是这样的——给更多数据，性能会好一点，但超过某个点之后就基本不再提高了。你的语音识别系统不会变得更准确，你的在线广告系统也不会更精准地展示最相关的广告，哪怕你喂给它更多数据。AI最近真正起飞，是因为神经网络和深度学习的崛起。用现代AI——神经网络和深度学习——我们发现，如果你训练一个小型神经网络，性能大概是这样，喂更多数据，性能在更长时间内持续提升。如果你训练一个更大的中型神经网络，性能会是这样。而如果你训练一个非常大的神经网络，性能就基本一直往上走。对于语音识别、在线广告、自动驾驶这些需要高性能、高准确率的应用来说，这让AI系统变得更好用，产品更被用户接受，对公司和用户来说价值都更大。

> So in summary, supervised learning just learns input output or A to B mappings. On one hand, input output A to B seems quite limiting, but when you find the right application scenario, this turns out to be incredibly valuable. Now the idea of supervised learning has been around for many decades, but it's really taken off in the last few years. Why is this? My friends ask me, hey Andrew, why is supervised learning taking off now? There's a picture I draw for them, and I want to show you this picture now, and you may be able to draw this picture for others that ask you the same question as well. Let's say on the horizontal axis, you plot the amount of data you have per task. So for speech recognition, this might be the amount of audio data and transcripts you have. In a lot of industries, the amount of data you have access to has really grown over the last couple of decades. So the rise of the internet, the rise of computers, a lot of what used to be say pieces of paper are now instead recorded on a digital computer. So we've just been getting more and more data. Now let's say on the vertical axis, you plot the performance of an AI system. It turns out that if you use a traditional AI system, then the performance would grow like this. That as you feed it more data, its performance gets a bit better, but beyond a certain point, it did not get that much better. AI has really taken off recently due to the rise of neural networks and deep learning. But with modern AI, with neural networks and deep learning, what we saw was that if you train a small neural network, then the performance kind of looks like this, whereas you feed it more data, performance keeps getting better for much longer. And if you train an even slightly larger neural network, say a medium-sized neural net, then the performance may look like that. And if you train a very large neural network, then the performance just kind of keeps on getting better and better. And for applications like speech recognition, online advertising, building a self-driving car, where having a high performance, highly accurate speech recognition system is important, this has enabled these AI systems to get much better and make speech recognition products much more acceptable to users, much more valuable to companies and to users.

### 这张图有两个重要含义：第一，要想达到最高的性能水平，你得有大量数据——这就是为什么你总听到"大数据"，更多数据几乎总是有帮助的；第二，你得能训练非常大的神经网络——快速计算机和GPU这类专用处理器的兴起，让很多公司（不只是科技巨头）都能在足够大的数据上训练大型神经网络，从而获得极好的性能并创造商业价值；事实上，这种"扩大数据量+扩大模型规模"的思路，也正是最近大语言模型等生成式AI取得突破的关键驱动力

> 这张图有两个重要含义。如果你想要最好的性能——让性能达到上面这个水平——你基本上需要两样东西。第一，大量数据真的很有帮助。这就是为什么你经常听到"大数据"这个词，更多数据几乎总是有帮助的。第二，你需要能够训练非常大的神经网络。所以快速计算机的兴起——包括摩尔定律——以及专用处理器（比如GPU，图形处理器，后面的视频会讲更多）的兴起，让很多公司——不只是那些巨型科技公司，而是很多很多其他公司——也能够在大规模数据上训练大型神经网络，从而获得非常好的性能并创造商业价值。事实上，也正是这种规模化的思路——增加数据量和模型规模——推动了大语言模型等生成式AI系统最近的突破。

> Now here are a couple of implications of this figure. If you want the best possible levels of performance, your performance to be up here, to hit this level of performance, then you kind of meet two things. One is it really helps to have a lot of data. So that's why sometimes you hear about big data, having more data almost always helps. And the second thing is you want to be able to train a very large neural network. And so the rise of fast computers, including Moore's law, but also the rise of specialized processors, such as graphics processors units or GPUs, which you hear more about in the later video, has enabled many companies, not just the giant tech companies, but many, many other companies to be able to train large neural nets on a large enough amount of data in order to get very good performance and drive business value. In fact, it was also this type of scaling, increasing the amount of data and the size of the models that was instrumental to the recent breakthroughs in training generative AI systems, including the large language models that we discussed just now.

### AI中最重要的思想就是机器学习和监督学习——也就是A到B、输入到输出的映射；让这一切真正有效运作的关键是数据；下节课我们就来看看什么是数据，你手上可能已经有哪些数据，以及如何把数据喂给AI系统

> AI中最重要的思想就是机器学习，特别是监督学习——也就是A到B、输入到输出的映射。让这一切真正有效运作的关键是数据。下节课我们就来看看什么是数据，你手上可能已经有哪些数据，以及如何思考把这些数据喂给AI系统。咱们接着看下一个视频。

> The most important idea in AI has been machine learning and specifically supervised learning, which means A to B or input output mappings. What enables it to work really well is data. In the next video, let's take a look at what is data and what data you might already have and how to think about feeding this into AI systems. Let's go on to the next video.


---

## 三、什么是数据——数据是AI的燃料，但获取数据有四种方式，使用数据有两个常见误区，而且数据往往是脏的，需要AI团队来清洗和处理

### 数据通常以表格（数据集）的形式存在——比如你想给房子定价，你可能收集一个电子表格，一列是房子面积，一列是价格，让AI学习从面积（A）到价格（B）的映射；但A和B的定义不是固定的——你可以把面积和卧室数量都作为A来预测价格B，也可以反过来：给定预算（A），预测能买多大面积的房子（B）；到底什么作A什么作B，完全取决于你的业务场景

> 你可能听说过数据对构建AI系统非常重要，但数据到底是什么？来看看。来看一个数据表格的例子——我们也叫它数据集。假如你想给要买卖的房子定价，你可能会收集这样一个数据集，就是一个普通的电子表格。一列是房子的大小，比如平方英尺或平方米，另一列是房子的价格。如果你想构建一个AI或机器学习系统来帮你给房子定价，或者判断一套房子价格是否合理，你可能会决定：房子的大小是A，价格是B，然后让AI学习这个从输入到输出的映射。但是，除了仅凭面积来定价，你可能会说：咱们也收集一下卧室数量的数据。那样的话，A可以是前两列，B就是价格。给定一个数据表格、一个数据集，到底什么是A什么是B，其实是由你和你的业务需求来决定的。这是一个房地产中介公司可能拥有的数据集的例子。由你来决定什么是A、什么是B，怎么选才能为你的业务创造价值。再比如，如果你有一个预算，想决定自己能负担多大的房子，那你可能会决定输入A是一个人的花费金额，B是房子的面积。这就是完全不同的A和B选择——告诉你给定预算，应该看多大面积的房子。

> Let's look at an example of a table of data, which we also call a data set. If you're trying to figure out how to price houses that you're trying to buy or sell, you might collect a data set like this, and this can be just a spreadsheet, like a mixed spreadsheet of data, where one column is the size of the house, say in square feet or square meters, and the second column is the price of the house. And so if you're trying to build an AI system or machine learning system to help you set prices for houses or figure out if a house is priced appropriately, you might decide that the size of the house is A, and the price of the house is B, and have an AI system learn this input to output or A to B mapping. Now rather than just pricing a house based on the size, you might say, well, let's also collect data on the number of bedrooms of this house. In that case, A can be both of these first two columns, and B can be just the price of the house. So given a table of data, given a data set, it's actually up to you, up to your business use case, to decide what is A and what is B. Data is often unique to your business, and this is an example of a data set that a real estate agency might have if they're trying to help price houses. And it's up to you to decide what is A and what is B, and how to choose these definitions of A and B to make it valuable for your business. As another example, if you have a certain budget and you want to decide what is the size of house you can afford, then you might decide that the input A is how much does someone spend, and B is just the size of the house in square feet. And that would be a totally different choice of A and B that tells you, given a certain budget, what's the size of the house you should be maybe looking at.

### 再看一个猫识别器的例子——输入A是一组图片，输出B是"是猫/不是猫"的标签；机器学习圈有个传统，特别喜欢用猫来举例，这可以追溯到吴恩达在Google Brain团队时做的那个著名的"Google猫"——一个通过看YouTube视频学会识别猫的AI系统；从此猫就成了机器学习的经典例子，他向所有爱狗人士表示歉意

> 这是另一个数据集的例子。假设你想构建一个AI系统来识别照片中的猫。我不太确定你为什么要这么做，也许是个好玩的手机App，你想给所有猫的照片打标签。你可能会收集这样一个数据集：输入A是一组不同的图片，输出B是标签——第一张是猫、第二张不是猫、第三张是猫、第四张不是猫。AI输入一张图片A，输出B——是猫还是不是猫？这样你就可以在相册或App里给所有猫的照片打标签了。机器学习圈有个传统，特别爱用猫。我想这大概是从我领导Google Brain团队时开始的——我们发表了一个有点出名的"Google猫"结果，一个AI系统通过看YouTube视频学会了识别猫。从那时起，机器学习领域就有了用猫作为例子的传统。我们向所有爱狗人士道歉——我也喜欢狗。

> Here's another example of a data set. Let's say that you want to build an AI system to recognize cats in pictures. I'm not sure why you might want to do that, but maybe the fun mobile app and you want to tag all the pictures of cats. So you might collect the data sets where the input A is set of different images and the output B are labels that says, first picture is a cat, there's not a cat, there's a cat, there's not a cat, and if an AI input a picture A and output B, is it a cat or not? So you can tag all the cat pictures on your photo feed or on your mobile app. In machine learning tradition, there's actually a lot of cats in machine learning. I think some of this started when I was leading the Google Brain team and we published the results with a somewhat infamous Google cat, where an AI system learned to detect cats from watching YouTube videos. But since then, there's been a tradition of using cats as a running example when talking about machine learning. We've apologized to all the dog lovers out there. I love dogs too.

### 获取数据有四种方式：第一种是人工标注——你收集一批图片，自己或请人一张张标注"是猫/不是猫"，虽然需要成百上千张图，但这是经过验证的可靠方法；第二种是观察用户行为——比如你运营一个电商网站，用户买东西或不买的过程自然就生成了数据（用户ID、时间、价格、是否购买）；第三种是观察机器行为——比如工厂里的大型机器，记录温度、压力、是否发生故障，用于预测性维护；第四种是从网上下载或从合作伙伴获取——互联网上有大量免费数据集，从计算机视觉、自动驾驶、语音识别到医学影像应有尽有，只要注意许可和版权

> 数据很重要，但你怎么获取数据呢？第一种方式是人工标注。比如你收集了一批图片，然后你自己或请人逐一浏览这些图片并标注：第一张是猫，第二张不是猫，第三张是猫——通过人工标注每张图片，你就有了一个用于构建猫检测器的数据集。当然你需要的不止四张图，可能需要成百上千张，但人工标注是获取同时有A和B的数据集的经过验证的可靠方法。第二种获取数据集的方式是观察用户行为或其他类型的行为。比如你运营一个卖东西的电商网站，你以不同价格向用户展示商品，你自然就能观察到他们是否购买。仅仅通过用户购买或不购买的行为，你就能收集到包含用户ID、访问时间、展示价格、是否购买的数据集。用户在使用你网站的过程中就帮你生成了这些数据。这是观察用户行为的例子。我们也可以观察其他东西的行为，比如机器。如果你在工厂里运营一台大型机器，想预测它是否即将发生故障，那仅仅通过观察机器的行为，你就可以记录下包含机器ID、温度、压力、是否故障的数据集。如果你的应用是预测性维护——你想判断一台机器是否即将出故障——那你可以选择这些数据作为输入A，那个作为输出B来做预防性维护。第三种非常常见的获取数据方式是从网站下载或从合作伙伴那里获取。感谢开放的互联网，有太多数据集可以免费下载——从计算机视觉、图像数据集到自动驾驶数据集，到语音识别数据集，到医学影像数据集，还有很多很多。如果你的应用需要某种数据类型，而且可以直接从网上免费下载（注意许可和版权），这可能是启动一个应用的很好方式。最后，如果你跟合作伙伴合作，比如你是一家工厂的合作方，他们可能已经收集了包含机器温度、压力、是否故障的大数据集可以给你。

> Data is important, but how do you get data? How do you acquire data? Well one way to get data is manual labeling. For example, you might collect a set of pictures, like these over here, and then you might either yourself or have someone else go through these pictures and label each of them. So the first one is a cat, the second one is not a cat, the third one is a cat, and by manually labeling each of these images, you now have a data set for building a cat detector. To do that, you actually need more than four pictures. You might need hundreds or thousands of pictures, but manual labeling is a tried and true way of getting a data set where you have both A and B. Another way to get a data set is from observing user behaviors or other types of behaviors. So for example, let's say you run a website that sells things online, so an e-commerce or an electronic commerce website, where you offer things to users at different prices and you can just observe if they buy your product or not. So just through the act of either buying or not buying your product, you may be able to collect a data set like this. We can store the user ID, the time the user visited your website, the price you offer the product to the users, as well as whether or not they purchased it. And so just by using your website, users can generate this data from you. This was an example of observing user behaviors. We can also observe behaviors of other things, such as machines. If you run a large machine in a factory and you want to predict if a machine is about to fail or have a fault, then just by observing the behavior of a machine, you can then record a data set like this. There's a machine ID, there's a temperature of the machine, there's a pressure within the machine, and then did the machine fail or not? And if your application is preventative maintenance, say you want to figure out if a machine is about to fail, then you could, for example, choose this as the input A and choose that as the output B to try to figure out if a machine is about to fail, in which case you might do preventative maintenance on the machine. The third and very common way of acquiring data is to download it from a website or to get it from a partner. Thanks to the open internet, there's just so many data sets that you can download freely, ranging from computer vision or image data sets to self-driving car data sets to speech recognition data sets to medical imaging data sets to many, many more. And so if your application needs a type of data to just download off the web, keeping in mind licensing and copyright, then that could be a great way to get started on an application. And finally, if you work with a partner, say you're working with a factory, then they may already have collected a big data set of machines and temperatures and pressure into the machines they or not that they could give to you.

### 数据很重要，但也有些被过度炒作，最常见的两个误区：第一个是CEO说"给我三年建IT团队，我们现在收集大量数据，三年后就有完美的数据集再做AI"——这是个很糟糕的策略；正确做法是一旦开始收集数据，就马上给AI团队看，因为AI团队可以告诉IT团队应该收集什么类型的数据、采集频率应该如何调整，比如"别每10分钟记录一次，改成一分钟一次，这样我们就能做出更好的预测性维护系统"；IT和AI团队之间存在来回反馈的互动关系

> 数据很重要，但它也有些被过度炒作，有时候还被用错。让我跟你说两个最常见的误区或错误的数据思维。我跟一些大公司CEO交流时，确实有几位对我说过：安德鲁，给我三年时间，我把IT团队建起来，我们正在收集海量数据，三年后我就会有完美的数据集，到那时再做AI。结果证明这是个非常糟糕的策略。相反，我给每家公司的建议是：一旦开始收集数据，就马上开始把它拿给AI团队看、喂给AI团队。因为通常AI团队能给你的IT团队反馈——应该收集什么类型的数据、应该继续建设什么样的IT基础设施。比如，AI团队看了看你工厂的数据后可能会说：嘿你知道吗，你们应该不只每10分钟记录一次这台大型制造机的数据，而是每分钟记录一次，这样我们就能为你们做出更好的预测性维护系统。所以IT和AI团队之间往往存在这种来回的互动。我的建议通常是尽早从AI团队那里获取反馈，因为这能帮你指导IT基础设施的发展方向。

> Data is important, but it's also a little bit overhyped and sometimes misused. Let me describe to you two of the most common misuses or the bad ways of thinking about data. When I speak of CEOs of large companies, a few of them have actually said to me, hey Andrew, give me three years to build up my IT team. We're collecting so much data. And then after three years, I'll have this perfect data set and then we'll do AI then. And it turns out that's a really bad strategy. Instead, what I recommend to every company is once you start to collecting some data, go ahead and start showing it or feeding it to an AI team because often the AI team can give feedback to your IT team on what types of data to collect and what types of IT infrastructure to keep on building. For example, maybe an AI team can look at your factory data and say, hey, you know what? You can collect data from this big manufacturing machine not just once every 10 minutes, but instead once every one minute, then we could do a much better job building a preventative maintenance system for you. So there's often this interplay of this back and forth between IT and AI teams. And my advice is usually try to get feedback from AI earlier because it can help you guide the development of your IT infrastructure.

### 第二个误区：CEO看到新闻说数据很重要，就说"我有好多数据，AI团队肯定能从中创造价值"——但事实不总是这样；更多数据通常比更少好，但千万别以为有几TB或几GB数据，AI团队就一定能把它变得有价值；有一个极端案例：一家公司收购了一连串医疗领域的公司，押注他们的数据会很有价值，结果几年过去了，工程师们还没搞明白怎么从这些数据中创造价值——所以不要为了数据而过度投资数据，除非让AI团队先看一遍，因为他们能帮你判断什么数据才是真正最有价值的

> 第二个数据误区。不幸的是，我看到一些CEO读到了数据重要性的新闻，然后说：嘿，我有这么多数据，AI团队肯定能从中创造价值。不幸的是，这不一定能行。更多数据通常比更少好，但我不会理所当然地认为，就因为你有很多TB或GB的数据，AI团队就一定能把它变得有价值。所以我的建议是：不要把数据扔给AI团队然后假设它会有价值。实际上，有一个极端案例：我看到一家公司收购了一整串医疗领域的公司，他们的假设是这些公司的数据会非常有价值。结果几年过去了——据我所知——工程师们还没搞明白怎么把这些数据利用起来、从中真正创造价值。所以有时候行，有时候不行。我不会为了数据本身而过度投资获取数据，除非同时也让AI团队来看一眼，因为他们能帮你判断什么数据才是真正最有价值的。

> Second misuse of data. Unfortunately, I've seen some CEOs read about the importance of data and the news and then say, hey, I have so much data, surely an AI team can make it valuable. And unfortunately, this doesn't always work out. More data is usually better than less data, but I wouldn't take it for granted just because you have many terabytes or gigabytes of data that an AI team can actually make that valuable. So my advice is don't throw data in AI team and assume it will be valuable. In fact, in one extreme case, I saw one company go and acquire a whole string of other companies in medicine on the thesis on the hypothesis that their data would be very valuable. And now a couple years later, far as I know, the engineers have not yet figured out how to take all this data and actually create value, all of it. So sometimes it works and sometimes it doesn't, but I would not over invest in just acquiring data for the sake of data until unless you're also getting an AI team to take a look at it because they can help guide you to think through what is the data that is actually the most valuable.

### 数据往往是脏的——"垃圾进、垃圾出"，如果数据不好，AI学到的东西也不准；常见的数据问题包括：错误标签（比如一套房子不可能只卖1美元）和缺失值（一堆"未知"）；你的AI团队需要想办法清洗数据或处理这些错误标签和缺失值；此外数据还分为结构化数据（住在电子表格里的数据）和非结构化数据（图片、音频、文本——人类很容易理解的数据类型），生成式AI主要用于生成非结构化数据，而监督学习对两种数据都能处理得很好

> 最后，数据往往是脏的。你可能听说过"垃圾进、垃圾出"这句话——如果数据不好，AI学到的也会是不准的东西。来看一些数据问题的例子。比如你有这个包含房屋面积、卧室数和价格的数据集。可能会有错误标签或就是错误的数据——这套房子大概率不可能卖0.001千美元，也就是1美元。数据也可能有缺失值，比如这里有一堆"未知"。你的AI团队需要想办法清洗数据或者处理这些错误标签和缺失值。此外，数据还有多种类型。比如你有时候听到图片、音频和文本——这些是人类很容易理解的数据类型，有个术语叫"非结构化数据"。有些AI技术可以处理图片来识别猫、处理音频来识别语音、或者处理文本来理解垃圾邮件。另一边也有像右边这样的数据集——这是结构化数据的例子，基本上就是住在巨型电子表格里的数据。处理非结构化数据的技术和处理结构化数据的技术有些不同。今天的生成式AI主要用于生成非结构化数据，比如文本、图片和音频，而不是结构化数据。相比之下，监督学习对这两种数据——非结构化和结构化数据——都能处理得很好。

> Finally, data is messy. You may have heard the phrase garbage in, garbage out. And if you have bad data, then the AI will learn inaccurate things. Here are some examples of data problems. Let's say you have this data set of sizes of houses, number of bedrooms and price. You can have incorrect labels or just incorrect data. For example, this house is probably not going to sell for $0.001,000, just $1. Or data can also have missing values such as we have here a whole bunch of unknown values. And so your AI team will need to figure out how to clean up the data or how to deal with these incorrect labels and or missing values. And there are also multiple types of data. For example, sometimes you hear about images, audio and text. These are types of data that humans find it very easy to interpret. There's a term for this. This is called unstructured data. And there's certain types of AI techniques that could work with images to recognize cats, the audio to recognize speech or text to understand the email spam. And then there are also data sets like the one on the right. This is an example of structured data. And that basically means data that lives in a giant spreadsheet. And the techniques for dealing with unstructured data are a little bit different than the techniques for dealing with structured data. Generative AI today is used primarily to generate unstructured data, such as text, images and audio, rather than structured data. In contrast, supervised learning can work very well for both of these types of data, unstructured data and structured data.

### 这集你学到了什么是数据、怎么不误用数据（比如在IT基础设施上过度投资却不验证它对AI应用是否真的有用），以及数据虽然是脏的，但一个好的AI团队能帮你处理这些问题；下集我们来梳理AI领域那些令人困惑的术语——AI、机器学习、数据科学到底是什么意思，让你能自信准确地和别人讨论这些概念

> 在这集视频中，你学到了什么是数据，也看到了怎么不误用数据——比如在IT基础设施上过度投资，寄希望于它未来对AI有用，却没有实际验证它对你真正想做的AI应用到底有没有价值。最后，你也看到了数据是脏的，但一个好的AI团队能帮你处理这些问题。现在，AI领域有套复杂的术语——人们张口闭口就是AI、机器学习、数据科学。下集视频我想跟你分享这些术语到底是什么意思，这样你就能自信、准确地跟别人讨论这些概念了。咱们进入下个视频。

> In this video, you learned what is data and you also saw how not to misuse data. For example, by over-investing in an IT infrastructure in the hope that it will be useful for AI in the future, but we're actually checking that it really will be useful for the AI applications you want to build. And finally, you saw data is messy, but a good AI team will be able to help you deal with all of these problems. Now, AI has a complicated terminology where people throw around terms like AI, machine learning, data science. What I want to do in the next video is share with you what these terms actually mean, so that you can confidently and accurately talk about these concepts with others. Let's go on to the next video.


---

## 四、人工智能术语解析——机器学习、数据科学、深度学习、神经网络到底是什么意思，它们之间是什么关系，搞清楚这些你就不会再被各种Buzzword搞晕了

### 机器学习是让计算机在没有被明确编程的情况下具备学习能力——这是Arthur Samuel几十年前下的定义，他造了一个下棋程序，下得比自己这个发明人还好；机器学习项目的结果通常是一套运行中的软件系统，比如输入房子的面积、卧室数、浴室数、是否翻新（A），自动输出预估价格（B），这套系统可以全天候运行服务成千上万用户

> 你可能听过很多AI术语——机器学习、数据科学、神经网络、深度学习。这些词到底什么意思？这个视频你会看到AI领域最重要概念的术语定义，这样你就能跟别人讨论它们，并开始思考怎么把这些东西应用到你的业务中。咱们开始吧。假设你有一个房产数据集，包含房子面积、卧室数、浴室数、是否新翻新以及价格。如果你想建一个手机App帮人给房子估价，那这些就是输入A，这个是输出B，这就是一个机器学习系统。具体来说，就是一个学习输入到输出、A到B映射的机器学习系统。机器学习通常产出一个运行中的AI系统——一段软件，不管白天黑夜，随时可以自动输入A（房子的这些属性）并输出B。如果你有一个AI系统在运行，服务几十、成百上千甚至数百万用户，那通常就是一个机器学习系统。

> You might have heard terminology from AI such as machine learning or data science or neural networks or deep learning. What do these terms mean? In this video, you'll see what is this terminology of the most important concepts of AI so that you will speak with others about it and start thinking how these things could apply in your business. Let's get started. Let's say you have a housing dataset like this with the size of the house, number of bedrooms, number of bathrooms, whether the house is newly renovated as well as the price. If you want to build a mobile app to help people price houses, so this would be the input A and this would be the output B, then this would be a machine learning system. In particular, it would be one of those machine learning systems that learns input to output or A to B mappings. Machine learning often results in a running AI system. There's a piece of software that any time of day, any time of night can automatically input A, these properties of a house and output B. If you have an AI system running, serving dozens or hundreds of thousands of millions of users, that's usually a machine learning system.

### 数据科学则是从数据中提取知识和洞见的科学——结果往往是一份PPT幻灯片，用来向高管总结结论、辅助商业决策；比如同样的房产数据，数据科学团队分析后可能得出："面积差不多的情况下，三卧比两卧贵很多"或者"新翻新的房子有15%的溢价"——这些洞见帮你决定是建两卧还是三卧、值不值得花钱翻新

> 相比之下，还有另一种你想做的事——让一个团队分析你的数据集来获取洞见。团队可能会得出这样的结论：嘿，你知道吗，如果两套房子面积差不多，三卧的比两卧的贵很多，即便面积一样。或者：你知道吗，新翻新的房子有15%的溢价。这些洞见能帮你做决策——比如面积差不多的情况下，你该建两卧还是三卧来最大化价值？或者值不值得投资翻新一套房子？这就是数据科学项目的例子。数据科学项目的输出是一组洞见，能帮你做商业决策，比如建什么类型的房子、是否投资翻新。

> In contrast, here's something else you might want to do, which is to have a team analyze your dataset in order to gain insights. So a team might come up with a conclusion like, hey, did you know if you have two houses of a similar size, of a similar square footage, if the house is three bedrooms, then they cost a lot more than the house with two bedrooms, even if the square footage is the same. Or did you know that newly renovated homes have a 15% premium and this could help you make decisions such as, given a similar square footage, do you want to build a two-bedroom or three-bedroom size in order to maximize value? Or is it worth an investment to renovate a home in the hope that the renovation increases the price you can sell a house for? So these would be examples of data science projects. Where the output of a data science project is a set of insights that can help you make business decisions, such as what type of house to build or whether to invest in renovation.

### 机器学习和数据科学的边界其实有点模糊，行业里用这些词也不太一致；但可以用在线广告行业的一个对比来理解：大广告平台都有一套AI系统，输入你和你可能看到的广告的信息（A），输出你会不会点击（B），这个系统24/7全天运行驱动营收——这是机器学习系统；而如果你分析数据发现"旅游行业不怎么买广告，但如果多派销售去找旅游公司，可以说服他们多用广告"——这个分析结论就是一个数据科学项目，结果是一个商业建议而不是一套运行中的软件

> 机器学习和数据科学这两个术语之间的边界其实有点模糊，即便在今天行业里这两个词的用法也不一致。但我这里给出的是最常用的定义——不过你不会发现所有人都严格遵守这些定义。更正式一点说：机器学习是"让计算机在没有被明确编程的情况下具备学习能力"的研究领域——这是Arthur Samuel几十年前的定义。Arthur Samuel是机器学习的先驱之一，他造了一个下棋程序，这个程序下棋下得比他自己这个发明人还好。所以机器学习项目通常产出的是运行中的软件——给定A，输出B。相比之下，数据科学是从数据中提取知识和洞见的科学。所以数据科学项目的输出往往是一份PPT幻灯片——总结结论供高管采取商业行动，或者总结结论供产品团队决定如何改进网站。让我给你一个在线广告行业的例子。今天所有大广告平台都有一套AI，能快速判断你最可能点击什么广告——这是一个机器学习系统，而且非常赚钱。AI系统输入关于你和广告的信息，输出你是否会点击。这些系统24/7运行，驱动着这些公司的营收。相反，我也在在线广告行业做过数据科学项目——分析数据后发现，比如：旅游行业不怎么买广告，但如果多派销售去找旅游公司，你可以说服他们多用广告。这就是一个数据科学项目的例子——数据科学的结论和结果是高管决定让销售团队花更多时间去联系旅游行业。所以在同一家公司里，你可能同时有机器学习项目和数据科学项目，两者都可以非常有价值。

> The boundaries between these two terms, machine learning and data science, are actually a little bit fuzzy and these terms are not used consistently, even in industry today. But what I'm giving here is maybe the most commonly used definitions of these terms, but you will not find universal adherence to these definitions. To formalize these two notions a bit more, machine learning is the field of study that gives computers the ability to learn without being explicitly programmed. This is a definition by Arthur Samuel many decades ago. Arthur Samuel was one of the pioneers of machine learning who was famous for building a checkers playing program that could play checkers even better than he himself, the inventor, could play the game. So a machine learning project will often result in a piece of software that runs, that outputs B given A. In contrast, data science is the science of extracting knowledge and insights from data. And so the output of a data science project is often a slide deck, the PowerPoint presentation that summarizes conclusions for executives to take business actions or that summarizes conclusions for a product team to decide how to improve a website. Let me give an example of machine learning versus data science in the online advertising industry. Today the large ad platforms all have a piece of AI that quickly tells them what's the ad you are most likely to click on. So that's a machine learning system and this turns out to be incredibly lucrative. AI systems that inputs information about you and about the ad and outputs, will you click on this or not. These systems are running 24 seven and these are machine learning systems that drive aggregate for these companies. So there's a piece of software that runs. In contrast, I've also done data science projects in the online advertising industry. If analyzing data tells you, for example, that the travel industry is not buying a lot of ads, but if you send more salespeople to sell ads to travel companies, you could convince them to use more advertising. Then that would be an example of a data science project and the data science conclusion, the results and executives deciding to ask the sales team to spend more time reaching out to the travel industry. So even in one company, you may have different machine learning and data science projects, both of which can be incredibly valuable.

### 什么是深度学习？假设你给房子估价，输入A是面积、卧室数、浴室数、是否翻新，要输出价格B——最高效的方法之一就是把A喂给中间这个"大东西"，然后它输出价格；这个中间的大东西就叫神经网络（也叫人工神经网络，以区别于你大脑里的生物神经网络）；这些圆圈叫人工神经元，它们在彼此之间传递信息，整个大神经网络本质上就是一个巨大的数学方程——告诉你给定输入A，怎么算出价格B

> 你可能也听过深度学习。什么是深度学习？假设你想预测房价、给房子估价。你有一个输入，告诉你面积、卧室数、浴室数、是否翻新。给定这个输入A，最有效的估价方法之一就是把它喂给中间这个东西，让它输出价格。中间这个大东西叫神经网络，有时候也叫人工神经网络——这是为了跟你大脑里的神经网络区分开。人类大脑由神经元组成，当我们说人工神经网络时，只是为了强调这不是生物大脑，而是一段软件。人工神经网络做的事情就是接受输入A（这四个属性），然后输出B（预估房价）。本周后面有一个可选视频，我会给你展示更多关于人工神经网络到底是什么的细节。但人类的全部认知，都是靠你大脑里的神经元传递电脉冲、传递小消息来实现的。当我们画一个人工神经网络的图时，跟大脑有一个非常粗略的类比。这些小圆圈叫人工神经元，或者简单就叫神经元。它们也在彼此之间传递信息。这个巨大的人工神经网络本质上就是一个巨大的数学方程——告诉你给定输入A，怎么计算出价格B。如果你觉得这里细节太多，别担心，后面会讲更多细节。但关键要记住的是：神经网络是学习A到B、输入到输出映射的非常有效的技术。

> You may have also heard of deep learning. So what is deep learning? Let's say you want to predict housing prices. You want to price houses. So you have an input that tells you the size of house number bedrooms and bathrooms and whether it's newly renovated. One of the most effective ways to price houses given this input A would be to feed it to this thing here in order to have it output the price. This big thing in the middle is called a neural network and sometimes you also call it an artificial neural network. And that's the distinguish it from the neural network that is in your brain. So the human brain is made up of neurons. And so when we say artificial neural network, that's just to emphasize that this is not the biological brain, but it says a piece of software. And what a neural network does when artificial neural network does is takes its input A, which is all of these four things, and then output B, which is the estimated price of the house. Now in a later optional video this week, I'll show you more what this artificial neural network really is. But all of human cognition is made up of neurons in your brain passing electrical impulses, passing little messages to each other. And when we draw a picture of an artificial neural network, there's a very loose analogy to the brain. And these little circles are called artificial neurons or just neurons for short. It also passes neurons to each other. And this big artificial neural network is just a big mathematical equation that tells it given the inputs A, how do you compute the price B? In case it seems like there are a lot of details here, don't worry about it. We'll talk more about these details later. But the key takeaway is that a neural network is a very effective technique for learning A to B or input output mappings.

### 今天"神经网络"和"深度学习"这两个词几乎可以互换使用，意思基本一样；几十年前这类软件就叫神经网络，但近年来大家发现"深度学习"这个词品牌听起来好得多，所以它火了起来；那神经网络跟人脑到底有多大关系？答案是几乎没关系——它最初确实受了人脑的启发，但具体工作原理跟生物大脑几乎完全不相关；吴恩达现在非常谨慎，不会在人工神经网络和人脑之间做什么类比，虽然确实有那么一点点启发渊源

> 今天，"神经网络"和"深度学习"这两个词几乎可以互换使用，它们的意思基本一样。几十年前，这类软件就叫神经网络，但近年来我们发现"深度学习"是个听起来好得多的品牌名，所以这个词就火了起来。那神经网络或者人工神经网络跟大脑到底有什么关系呢？答案是几乎没关系。神经网络最初确实是受大脑启发的，但它的具体工作原理跟生物大脑的工作方式几乎完全无关。所以今天我非常谨慎，不会在人工神经网络和生物大脑之间做任何类比——虽然确实存在那么一点粗略的启发渊源。

> And today the terms neural network and deep learning are used almost interchangeably. They mean essentially the same thing. Many decades ago, this type of software was called a neural network. But in recent years, we found that deep learning was just a much better sounding brand. And so that for better words is the term that's been taken off recently. So what do neural networks or artificial neural networks have to do with the brain? It turns out almost nothing. Neural networks were originally inspired by the brain, but the details of how they work are almost completely unrelated to how biological brains work. So I choose very cautious today about making any analogies between artificial neural networks and the biological brain, even though there was some loose inspiration there.

### AI有很多工具——除了机器学习、数据科学、深度学习这些核心概念外，媒体还会提到生成式AI、无监督学习、强化学习、图模型、规划、知识图谱等等；你不需要知道所有术语，它们只是让计算机变得智能的各种工具；如果画一个维恩图：AI是最大的集合，里面最大的一块是机器学习，机器学习中当今最重要的部分是神经网络/深度学习，但机器学习里也有非深度学习的其他工具；数据科学则是一个跨领域的子集，它用到很多AI、机器学习和深度学习的工具，但也有自己独立的工具来解决商业洞见这类重要问题

> AI有很多不同的工具。这个视频你学到了什么是机器学习、什么是数据科学，还有什么是深度学习、什么是神经网络。你可能在媒体上也会听到其他流行词，比如生成式AI、无监督学习、强化学习、图模型、规划、知识图谱等等。你不需要知道所有这些词的意思，它们只是让计算机变得智能的另外一些工具。后面的视频我会试着给你讲讲其中一些术语的大概意思。但我希望你能记住的最重要的工具和术语是：机器学习和数据科学，以及深度学习和神经网络——这是一种非常强大的做机器学习（有时也做数据科学）的方法。如果画一张维恩图来展示这些概念之间的关系，大概是这样：AI是一个巨大的工具集，用于让计算机变得智能。AI中最大的子集大概就是机器学习的工具，但AI也确实有其他不是机器学习的工具——比如下面列出的这些流行词。而在机器学习中，当今最重要的部分是神经网络或深度学习——这是做监督学习（A到B映射）以及其他一些事情的非常强大的工具集。但机器学习里也有其他不是深度学习的工具。那数据科学怎么放进这张图？行业里用词不太一致。有人会说数据科学是AI的子集，有人会说AI是数据科学的子集——取决于你问谁。但我会说，数据科学可能是一个跨领域的子集，它用到AI、机器学习和深度学习的很多工具，但也有自己独立的其他工具，用来解决推动商业洞见这一类非常重要的问题。

> So AI has many different tools. In this video, you learned about what are machine learning and data science, and also what is deep learning and what's a neural network. You might also hear in the media other buzzwords like generative AI, unsupervised learning, reinforcement learning, graphical models, planning, knowledge graphs, and so on. You don't need to know what all of these other terms mean, but these are just other tools like getting AI systems to meet computers act intelligently. I'll try to give you a sense of what some of these terms mean in later videos as well. But the most important tools and terms I hope you remember from this are machine learning and data science, as well as deep learning and neural networks, which are a very powerful way to do machine learning and sometimes data science. If we were to draw a Venn diagram showing how all these concepts fit together, this is what it might look like. AI is this huge set of tools for making computers behave intelligently. Of AI, the biggest subset is probably tools from machine learning, but AI does have other tools than machine learning, such as some of these buzzwords listed at the bottom. And of machine learning, the part of machine learning that's most important these days is neural networks or deep learning, which is a very powerful set of tools for carrying out supervised learning or A to B mappings as well as some of the things. But there are also other machine learning tools that are not just deep learning tools. So how does data science fit into this picture? There is inconsistency in how the technologies use. Some people will tell you data science is a subset of AI. Some people will tell you AI is a subset of data science. It depends upon who you ask. But I would say that data science is maybe a cross cutting subset of all of these tools that uses many tools from AI machine learning and deep learning, but has some other separate tools as well that solves a very set of important problems in driving business insights.

### 这集你了解了机器学习、数据科学、深度学习和神经网络的含义，希望这些能让你对AI最常见、最重要的术语有个清晰的理解，并开始思考怎么把这些东西用到你自己的公司里；下集我们来聊聊：到底什么能让一家公司真正擅长AI？

> 这集视频中，你看到了什么是机器学习、什么是数据科学，以及什么是深度学习和神经网络。我希望这能让你对AI中最常见、最重要的术语有个清晰的理解，并开始思考怎么把这些东西用到你自己的公司里。那么，到底什么能让一家公司真正擅长AI？咱们下集聊聊。

> In this video, you saw what is machine learning, what is data science, and what is deep learning and neural networks. I hope this gives you a sense of the most common and important terminology using AI and you can start thinking about how these things might apply to your company. Now, what does it mean for a company to be good at AI? Let's talk about that in the next video.

## 五、人工智能公司的核心要素——用几个神经网络不叫AI公司，真正的AI公司是系统性做好AI让你能做的事：战略数据获取、统一数据仓库、发现自动化机会、引入新角色，以及一套五步AI转型指南

### 互联网时代教给我们一个重要教训：给你最爱的购物中心建个网站、在上面卖东西，这本身并不把它变成互联网公司；真正的互联网公司会做那些"互联网让你能做的事"——比如普及A/B测试（随时上线两个版本看哪个更好，传统购物中心可没法在平行宇宙开两家店），迭代周期极短（每周甚至每天上新功能，而购物中心改造一次要几个月），以及把决策权下放给工程师和产品经理（而不是CEO一个人拍板——因为只有工程师和产品经理才足够了解技术、产品和用户）

> 什么让一家公司擅长AI？也许更重要的是，你的公司要怎么样才能变得擅长用AI？我之前领导过Google Brain团队和百度的AI团队，分别帮Google和百度成为了优秀的AI公司。那你能为自己的公司做什么呢？我从互联网的崛起中学到了一个教训，我认为这对所有人在AI崛起中找准方向都很重要。来看看。互联网时代教给我们的一个教训是：如果你拿你最喜欢的购物中心——比如我和我太太有时候去Stanford购物中心逛街——你给它建一个网站，在上面卖东西，这本身并不把这个购物中心变成一家互联网公司。事实上，几年前我跟一家大型零售公司的CEO聊天，他对我说：安德鲁，我有个网站，我在网站上卖东西；亚马逊也有个网站，亚马逊也在网站上卖东西——一样的嘛。但当然不一样。有网站的购物中心跟一流的互联网公司根本不是一回事。那到底是什么定义了一家互联网公司，如果不是"是否在网站上卖东西？"我认为互联网公司是那些"把互联网让你能做的事做得特别好"的公司。比如，互联网公司进行普及化的A/B测试——也就是说我们习惯性地同时上线两个不同版本的网站，看哪个效果更好，因为我们能做得到——这样我们学习的速度快得多。而传统购物中心很难在两个平行宇宙同时运营两家店，你可能只能每个季度或每半年做一些改动。互联网公司的迭代周期往往非常短，你可以每周甚至每天发布新功能——因为你能做得到。而购物中心只能每隔几个月重新设计和改造一次。互联网公司也倾向于把决策权从CEO下放到工程师和其他专业角色比如产品经理手中。这跟传统购物中心形成对比——传统模式是CEO拍板所有关键决策，然后所有人按CEO说的做。结果证明传统模式在互联网时代行不通，因为只有工程师和产品经理这类专业角色才足够了解技术、产品和用户，才能做出好决策。所以这些就是互联网公司为了"把互联网让你能做的事做好"而做的事。这是从互联网时代学到的教训。

> A lesson we learned from the rise of the internet was that if you take your favorite shopping mall, so my wife and I sometimes shop at Stanford Shopping Center, and you build a website for the shopping mall, maybe sell things on the website, that by itself does not turn the shopping mall into an internet company. In fact, a few years ago, I was speaking with the CEO of a large retail company who said to me, hey Andrew, I have a website, I sell things on the website, Amazon has a website, Amazon sells things on the website is the same thing. But of course it wasn't, and the shopping mall with a website isn't the same thing as a first class internet company. So what is it that defines an internet company if it isn't just whether or not you sell things on the website? I think an internet company is a company that does the things the internet lets you do really well. For example, we engage in pervasive A/B testing, meaning we routinely throw up two different versions of the website and see which one works better because we can, and so we learn much faster. Whereas in a traditional shopping mall, it's very difficult to have two shopping malls in two parallel universes, and you can only maybe change things around every quarter or every six months. Internet companies tend to have very short iteration times. You can ship a new product every week or maybe even every day because you can. Whereas a shopping mall can be redesigned and re-architected only every several months. Internet companies also tend to push decision making down from the CEO to the engineers and to other specialized roles such as the product managers. This is in contrast to a traditional shopping mall where you can maybe have the CEO just decide all the key decisions and then just everyone does what the CEO says. And it turns out that traditional model doesn't work in the internet era because only the engineers and other specialized roles like product managers know enough about the technology and the product and the users to make great decisions. So these are some of the things that internet companies do in order to make sure they do the things that the internet lets them do really well. This is a lesson we learned from the internet era.

### AI时代同理：今天你可以让任何公司用几个神经网络或深度学习算法，但这本身并不会把它变成AI公司；真正的AI公司（有时候叫"AI优先公司"）是那些"把AI让你能做的事做得特别好"的公司——比如战略数据获取，很多大型消费科技公司会推出不赚钱的免费产品来获取数据，然后在别的地方变现，吴恩达亲自带过战略团队，专门推出不盈利的产品仅仅为了获取数据

> AI时代呢？我认为，今天你可以让任何公司用几个神经网络或几个深度学习算法，但这本身并不会把公司变成AI公司。相反，是什么造就了一家优秀的AI公司——有时候叫"AI优先公司"——是你是不是在"把AI让你能做的事做得特别好"？比如，AI公司非常擅长战略数据获取。这就是为什么很多大型消费科技公司会有不赚钱的免费产品——因为这些产品让他们能获取数据，然后在别的地方变现。我亲自带过战略团队，我们会有意推出完全不赚钱的产品，目的仅仅是为了获取数据。思考"怎么获取数据"是优秀AI公司的关键能力之一。

> How about the AI era? I think that today you can take any company and have it use a few neural networks or a few deep learning algorithms that by itself does not turn the company into an AI company. Instead what makes a great AI company sometimes an AI first company is are you doing the things that AI lets you do really well? For example, AI companies are very good at strategic data acquisition. This is why many of the large consumer tech companies may have free products that do not monetize and it allows them to acquire data that they can monetize elsewhere. So let strategy teams where we would deliberately launch products that do not make any money just for the sake of data acquisition. And thinking through how to get data is a key part of the great AI companies.

### AI公司往往拥有统一的数据仓库——如果你有50个不同的数据库或数据仓库分别由50个不同副总裁掌控，那工程师根本没法把数据汇聚起来"连点成线"发现规律；所以很多优秀的AI公司会提前投资，把数据汇集到单一数据仓库中，增加团队把不同线索串联起来的概率，当然前提是遵守隐私保护和GDPR等数据法规

> AI公司往往拥有统一的数据仓库。如果你有50个不同的数据库或50个不同的数据仓库，分别由50个不同副总裁掌管，那工程师根本不可能把数据汇聚到一个地方去"连点成线"、发现规律。所以很多优秀的AI公司会提前投资，把数据汇集到单一数据仓库中，以增加团队能把不同线索串联起来的概率。当然这一切的前提是遵守隐私保护以及像欧洲GDPR这样的数据法规。

> AI companies tend to have unified data warehouses. If you have 50 different databases or 50 different data warehouses under the control of 50 different vice presidents, then it will be impossible for an engineer to get the data into one place so that they can connect the dots and spot the patterns. So many great AI companies have preemptively invested in bringing the data together into single data warehouse to increase the odds that the teams can connect the dots. Subjective course to privacy guarantees and also to data regulations such as GDPR in Europe.

### AI公司还擅长发现自动化机会——善于判断"这里可以塞进一个监督学习算法的A到B映射，让人不用再做这个任务，而是自动完成"；AI公司也引入了很多新角色，比如机器学习工程师（MLE），以及划分团队任务的新方式；简单说，让公司擅长AI，就是把公司架构设计成"把AI让你能做的事做好"的样子

> AI公司非常擅长发现自动化机会——我们很善于发现：哦，这里可以插入一个监督学习算法，用一个A到B的映射来搞定，这样就不需要人来做这些任务了，我们可以自动化它。AI公司也有很多新角色，比如MLE——机器学习工程师——以及把任务拆分给团队成员的新方式。所以，要让你公司擅长AI，意味着要把公司的架构设计成"把AI让你能做的事做好"的样子。

> AI companies are very good at spotting automation opportunities. We're very good at seeing, oh, let's insert a supervised learning algorithm and have an A to B mapping here so that we don't have to have people do these tasks instead we can automate it. AI companies also have many new roles such as the MLE or machine learning engineer and new ways of dividing up tasks among different members of a team. So for a company to become good at AI means architecting the company to do the things that AI makes it possible to do really well.

### 让公司变得擅长AI需要一个过程，这不是什么神秘魔法——十年前Google、百度、Facebook、微软也不是今天这样的AI公司；吴恩达提出了一套系统化的"五步AI转型指南"：第一步，执行试点项目来获得动力，先做几个小项目感受AI能做什么、做AI项目是什么体验，可以在内部做也可以外包；第二步，建立内部AI团队并提供广泛的AI培训——不只是培训工程师，还要培训经理、部门负责人和高管，教会他们如何思考AI

> 让一家公司变得擅长AI确实需要一个过程。事实上，十年前，Google和百度——以及像Facebook和微软这样的公司——也并不是今天这样的优秀AI公司。那一家公司怎么变得擅长AI呢？事实证明，变得擅长AI并不是什么神秘的魔法过程。相反，有一套系统化的流程，几乎任何大公司都可以通过它变得擅长AI。这就是我推荐给那些想有效使用AI的公司的"五步AI转型指南"。我先在这里做一个简要概述，后面某一周会详细展开。第一步：执行试点项目来获得动力。就是先做几个小项目，更好地感受AI能做什么、不能做什么，以及做AI项目到底是什么体验。这可以在内部做，也可以外包团队来做。但最终你需要进入第二步：建立内部AI团队，并提供广泛的AI培训——不只是培训工程师，还要培训经理、部门负责人和高管，教会他们如何思考AI。

> Now for a company to become good at AI does require a process. In fact, 10 years ago, Google and Baidu as well as companies like Facebook and Microsoft that was not a part of were not great AI companies the way that they are today. So how can a company become good at AI? It turns out that becoming good at AI is not a mysterious magical process. Instead, there is a systematic process through which many companies almost any big company can become good at AI. This is the five step AI transformation playbook that I recommend to companies that want to become effective at using AI. I'll give a brief overview of the playbook here and then go into detail in a later week. Step one is to execute pilot projects to gain momentum. So just do a few small projects to get a better sense of what AI can and cannot do and get a better sense of what doing an AI project feels like. And this you could do in-house or you can also do with an outsourced team. But eventually you then need to do step two, which is to build an in-house AI team and provide broad AI training, not just to the engineers, but also to the managers, division leaders and executives on how to think about AI.

### 第三步是制定AI战略——在做完或正在做前两步的过程中，你对AI有了更深的理解，这时候制定战略才有意义；第四步是对齐内部和外部的沟通——确保所有利益相关方，从员工、客户到投资人，都对公司如何应对AI崛起有一致的理解；AI已经在软件行业创造了巨大价值，未来也将在软件行业之外创造巨大价值，如果你能帮你的公司变得擅长AI，你就能在创造这些价值中扮演领导角色

> 在做完这些或者在做这些的过程中，你对AI有了更深的了解，这时候对很多公司来说，制定AI战略就很重要了。最后，要对齐内部和外部的沟通——确保你所有的利益相关方，从员工、客户到投资人，都对你公司如何应对AI的崛起有一致的理解。AI已经在软件行业创造了巨大价值，并将继续如此。它也将在软件行业之外创造巨大价值。如果你能帮你的公司变得擅长AI，我希望你能在创造这些价值中扮演领导角色。

> After doing this or as you're doing this, you have a better sense of what AI is and then is important for many companies to develop an AI strategy. And finally to align internal and external communications so that all your stakeholders from employees, customers and investors are aligned with how your company is navigating the rise of AI. AI has created tremendous value in the software industry and will continue to do so. It will also create tremendous value outside the software industry. If you can help your company become good at AI, I hope you can play a leading role in creating a lot of this value.

### 这集你看到了什么让一家公司成为优秀的AI公司，也简要了解了AI转型指南——后面有更详细的内容；网上也有一份公开发布的AI转型指南文档，对这五步有更详细的说明；接下来面临的挑战是：做AI项目（比如第一步的试点项目）之前，你必须先理解AI能做什么、不能做什么——下集就来通过具体例子帮你建立直觉

> 这集视频中，你看到了什么让一家公司成为优秀的AI公司，也简要了解了AI转型指南——后面某一周我们会详细展开，作为帮公司变得擅长AI的路线图。如果你感兴趣，网上也有一份公开发布的AI转型指南文档，对这五步有更详细的说明，不过后面几周你也会看到更多细节。现在，做AI项目（比如第一步的试点项目）的一个关键挑战是：理解AI能做什么、不能做什么。下集视频我想通过一些例子，帮你建立关于AI能力和局限的直觉，这样你就能更好地为公司选择可能有效的AI项目。咱们接着看下个视频。

> In this video, you saw what is it that makes a company a good AI company and also briefly the AI transformation playbook, which are going to much greater detail on in a later week as a roadmap for helping companies become great at AI. If you're interested, there is also published online an AI transformation playbook that goes into these five steps in greater detail, but you'll see more of these in a later week as well. Now, one of the challenges of doing AI projects, such as the pilot projects in step one is understanding what AI can and cannot do. In the next video, I want to show you and give you some examples of what AI can and cannot do to help you better select projects for AI that may be effective for your company. Let's go on to the next video.
